      27: {
      28:     c->done = 0;
      29:     c->lock = IOLockAlloc();
      30: }
      31: 
      32: static inline void complete(struct completion *c)
      33: {
      34:     IOLockLock(c->lock);
      35:     c->done++;
      36:     IOLockWakeup(c->lock, (void *)c, false);
      37:     IOLockUnlock(c->lock);
      38: }
      39: 
      40: static inline void complete_all(struct completion *c)
      41: {
      42:     IOLockLock(c->lock);
      43:     c->done = ~0u;
      44:     IOLockWakeup(c->lock, (void *)c, false);
      45:     IOLockUnlock(c->lock);
      46: }

=== driver/RTL88WiFi/src/compat/linux/dma-mapping.h | bytes=5265 | sha256=27120e81f01b6b35ae209318cd0ffbac02e96a5b9cb73957dfcb0b12082af630 | git_tracked=no ===
[DMA_GLOBALS] matches=15
  ...
      14: struct device;  /* forward decl */
      15: 
      16: /*
      17:  * On macOS, DMA coherent memory is allocated by the kext using
      18:  * IOBufferMemoryDescriptor (kIOMemoryPhysicallyContiguous).
      19:  * The C driver code calls these helpers; the kext installs rtw88_dma_ops
      20:  * before probe so the fallback path never runs in production.
      21:  */
      22: struct rtw88_dma_alloc_ops {
      23:     void      *(*alloc_coherent)(struct device *dev, size_t size,
      24:                                   dma_addr_t *dma_handle, gfp_t flag);
      25:     void       (*free_coherent)(struct device *dev, size_t size,
      26:                                  void *cpu_addr, dma_addr_t dma_handle);
      27:     dma_addr_t (*map_single)(struct device *dev, void *ptr,
      28:                               size_t size, int direction);
  ...
      32:                                        size_t size, int dir);
      33:     void       (*sync_single_for_device)(struct device *dev, dma_addr_t addr,
      34:                                           size_t size, int dir);
      35: };
      36: 
      37: extern struct rtw88_dma_alloc_ops *rtw88_dma_ops;
      38: 
      39: static inline void *dma_alloc_coherent(struct device *dev, size_t size,
      40:                                         dma_addr_t *dma_handle, gfp_t flag)
      41: {
      42:     if (rtw88_dma_ops && rtw88_dma_ops->alloc_coherent)
      43:         return rtw88_dma_ops->alloc_coherent(dev, size, dma_handle, flag);
      44:     /* Fallback: plain kernel allocation (not DMA-safe) */
      45:     void *ptr = kzalloc(size, GFP_KERNEL);
      46:     if (ptr) *dma_handle = (dma_addr_t)(uintptr_t)ptr;
      47:     return ptr;

      98:  * (which corrupts the TX ring state — wp advances, HW chews garbage and
      99:  * advances rp, eventually deadlocking at wp+1 == rp). */
     100: static inline bool dma_mapping_error(struct device *dev, dma_addr_t addr)

    3670: SInt32 RTL88WiFi::stopDMA()
    3671: {
    3672:     /* This callback can run with the IO80211 command gate held. Do not join
    3673:      * workers or recursively disable the interface here: they may need that
    3674:      * same gate. Fence device DMA before acknowledging the request. Rings stay
    3675:      * allocated; enable() restores bus mastering for a reversible stop. */
    3676:     __atomic_store_n(&_dmaStopped, true, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
    3677:     if (_intrSrc) _intrSrc->disable();
    3678:     if (_pciDev) {
    3679:         _pciDev->setBusMasterEnable(false);
    3680:         if (_pciDev->configRead16(kIOPCIConfigCommand) & 0x0004)
    3681:             return kIOReturnError;
    3682:     }
    3683:     return kIOReturnSuccess;
    3684: }
    3685: 
    3686: void RTL88WiFi::systemWillShutdown(IOOptionBits specifier)
    3687: {
    3688:     if (specifier == kIOMessageSystemWillPowerOff ||
    3689:         specifier == kIOMessageSystemWillRestart) {
    3690:         __atomic_store_n(&_shutdown, true, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
    3691:         const SInt32 result = stopDMA();
    3692:         IOLog("RTL88WiFi: shutdown DMA fence result=0x%x\n", result);
    3693:     }
    3694:     super::systemWillShutdown(specifier);
    3695: }

    1883:     if (skb->len < hdrlen) { kfree_skb(skb); return; }

    1888:     /* A protected frame the hardware did not decrypt is still ciphertext;
    1889:      * mac80211 would drop it. Log counts 1, 2, 4, 8, ... */

     574: IOOutputQueue *RTW88PCIDevice::createOutputQueue()

     580:      * descriptor slot from ring->r.wp and fills it BEFORE taking irq_lock
     581:      * (only the wp increment is locked), so two concurrent submissions fill the
     582:      * same slot and skip the next, leaving a zeroed descriptor in the BE ring.

     601:      * rtw_pci_tx_write_data() would return -ENOSPC and rtw_tx() would FREE the
     602:      * skb — silently dropping it.  Under a sustained transfer those dropped
     603:      * frames (TCP ACKs/data) stall the connection.  Returning
     604:      * kIOReturnOutputStall makes IOGatedOutputQueue hold this exact packet and
     605:      * stop dispatching; resumeTxIfStalled() (fired from the IRQ bottom-half

     106: /* DMA ops shim */
     107: static void *compat_dma_alloc(struct device *dev, size_t size,
     108:                                dma_addr_t *dma_handle, gfp_t flag)
     109: {
     110:     if (!g_pci_dev_instance) return nullptr;
     111:     IOPhysicalAddress phys = 0;
     112:     void *virt = g_pci_dev_instance->allocCoherent(size, &phys);
     113:     if (dma_handle) *dma_handle = (dma_addr_t)phys;
     114:     return virt;
     115: }
     116: static void compat_dma_free(struct device *dev, size_t size,
     117:                              void *cpu_addr, dma_addr_t dma_handle)
     118: {
     119:     if (g_pci_dev_instance)
     120:         g_pci_dev_instance->freeCoherent(size, cpu_addr,
     121:                                           (IOPhysicalAddress)dma_handle);
     122: }
     123: static dma_addr_t compat_dma_map(struct device *dev, void *ptr,
     124:                                    size_t size, int dir)
     125: {
     126:     /*
     127:      * Always bounce: allocate a physically-contiguous <4GB buffer, record the
     128:      * original CPU VA in orig_va, and hand the chip the bounce PA.
     129:      *
  ...
     148:     if (dir == 1 /* DMA_TO_DEVICE */ || dir == 2 /* DMA_BIDIRECTIONAL */)
     149:         memcpy(bounce, ptr, size);
     150: 
     151:     return (dma_addr_t)phys;
     152: }
     153: static void compat_dma_unmap(struct device *dev, dma_addr_t addr,
     154:                                size_t size, int dir)
     155: {
     156:     /* Free the bounce buffer; freeCoherentByPhys is a no-op if not found */
     157:     if (g_pci_dev_instance)
     158:         g_pci_dev_instance->freeCoherentByPhys((IOPhysicalAddress)addr);
     159: }
     160: static void compat_dma_sync_cpu(struct device *dev, dma_addr_t addr,
     161:                                   size_t size, int dir)
     162: {
     163:     /*
     164:      * RX: chip finished writing to the bounce buffer.  Copy it into the
     165:      * original skb->data buffer so the driver can read it from there.
     166:      */
     167:     if (dir == 0 /* DMA_FROM_DEVICE */ && g_pci_dev_instance)
     168:         g_pci_dev_instance->syncBounceForCpu((IOPhysicalAddress)addr, size);
     169: }
     170: static void compat_dma_sync_dev(struct device *dev, dma_addr_t addr,
     171:                                   size_t size, int dir)
     172: {
     173:     /* Re-arm for next DMA: bounce is already in place, nothing to do */
     174: }

     876: void RTW88PCIDevice::syncBounceForCpu(IOPhysicalAddress dma, size_t size)
     877: {
     878:     /*
     879:      * Called by dma_sync_single_for_cpu(DMA_FROM_DEVICE) after the chip
     880:      * has finished writing received packet data into the bounce buffer.
     881:      * Copy bounce → original skb->data so the driver can parse the packet.
     882:      */
[DMA_ORDER] matches=2
  ...
     127:      * Always bounce: allocate a physically-contiguous <4GB buffer, record the
     128:      * original CPU VA in orig_va, and hand the chip the bounce PA.
     129:      *
     130:      * DMA_TO_DEVICE (1): copy ptr→bounce now; chip reads from bounce.
     131:      * DMA_FROM_DEVICE (0): don't copy now; chip writes to bounce.
     132:      *   dma_sync_single_for_cpu() will copy bounce→ptr after chip is done.
     133:      *
     134:      * This is the standard software bounce-buffer strategy.  It costs a memcpy
     135:      * per RX packet but is always correct regardless of where IOMalloc places
     136:      * the skb data pages.
     137:      */
     138:     if (!g_pci_dev_instance || !ptr) return 0;
  ...
     874: }

     877: {

     235: bool RTW88WCLInfraInterface::prepareRegistrationInfo(const ether_addr &address)
     236: {
     237:     using namespace RTW88WCLRegistrationInfo24H32;
     238: 
     239:     clearRegistrationInfo();
     240:     auto *info = reinterpret_cast<IOSkywalkEthernetInterface::RegistrationInfo *>(
     241:         _registrationInfo.bytes);
     242: 
     243:     /* 24H32 initRegistrationInfo requires option 1 and the exact 0x130 size.
     244:      * It supplies the other opaque slots through this interface's virtual
     245:      * hooks; do not replace it with a guessed local structure initializer.
     246:      */
     247:     if (!initRegistrationInfo(info, 1, sizeof(_registrationInfo.bytes))) {
     248:         clearRegistrationInfo();
     249:         return false;
     250:     }

     253:         !matchesApple24H32Template(_registrationInfo, address.octet)) {
     254:         clearRegistrationInfo();
     255:         return false;
     256:     }
     257: 
     258:     _registrationInfoPrepared = true;
     259:     return true;

       1: /* SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-only
       2:  * Fixed-capacity lock-free MPMC ring for owned pointers (mbufs or Skywalk packets).
       3:  *
       4:  * The ring does not allocate after reset(). It is safe for concurrent producers
       5:  * and one or more consumers. reset() is lifecycle-only and must run after every
       6:  * producer and consumer has been quiesced. A successful push transfers pointer
       7:  * ownership to the ring; a failed push leaves ownership with the caller.
       8:  */

     113: void RTW88WCLSkywalkTxAdapter::backendCompletion(
     114:     void *context, void *cookie, bool transmitted)
     115: {
     116:     auto *self = static_cast<RTW88WCLSkywalkTxAdapter *>(context);
     117:     auto *ticket = static_cast<TicketPool::Ticket *>(cookie);
     118:     if (!self || !ticket)
     119:         return;
     120: 
     121:     /* No Skywalk API, OSObject release, allocation, or blocking operation is
     122:      * allowed here. A deferred consumer performs the completion handoff. */
     123:     (void)TicketPool::complete(ticket, transmitted);
     124: }
     125: 
     126: uint32_t RTW88WCLSkywalkTxAdapter::drainCompletions(
     127:     CompletionSink sink, void *context)
     128: {
     129:     if (!sink)
     130:         return 0;
     131: 
     132:     /* A sink may trigger owner teardown; keep this adapter alive until the
     133:      * drain loop has stopped touching its ticket pool. */
     134:     retain();
     135:     uint32_t drained = 0;
     136:     for (;;) {
     137:         TicketPool::Ticket *ticket = _tickets.claimCompleted();
     138:         if (!ticket)
     139:             break;
     140: 
     141:         auto *packet = static_cast<IOSkywalkPacket *>(TicketPool::packet(ticket));
     142:         const bool transmitted = TicketPool::transmitted(ticket);
     143: 
  ...
     154:     }
     155:     release();
     156:     return drained;
     157: }
     158: 
     159: bool RTW88WCLSkywalkTxAdapter::hasOutstandingCompletions() const
     160: {
     161:     return _tickets.hasOutstanding();
     162: }
     163: 
     164: uint32_t RTW88WCLSkywalkTxAdapter::outstandingCompletionCount() const
     165: {
     166:     return _tickets.outstandingCount();
     167: }
  ...
     169: void RTW88WCLSkywalkTxAdapter::free()
     170: {
     171:     /* A per-ticket retain prevents free() while hardware callbacks are still
     172:      * pending. The owning controller must drain every completion before its
     173:      * final reference is released. */
     174:     if (_tickets.hasOutstanding())
     175:         IOLog("rtl88wifi-wcl: TX adapter free attempted with outstanding tickets\n");
     176:     unbind();
     177:     super::free();
     178: }

=== driver/RTL88WiFi/src/kext/wcl24h32/RTW88WCLTxCompletionSlots.hpp | bytes=4323 | sha256=af7a498d3b5d2e4855609533bd7b823e40a5ef6c83e27c76abfe2e497f7c8f7f | git_tracked=no ===
[SKYWALK_LIFETIME] matches=8
  ...
       1: /* SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-only
       2:  * Fixed-capacity completion tickets for the native WCL TX adapter.
       3:  *
       4:  * Multiple RTW88 completion contexts may publish concurrently. The Skywalk
       5:  * packet operations are deliberately kept out of complete(); a workloop-side
       6:  * consumer claims completed tickets and performs the deferred queue handoff.
       7:  */
       8: #pragma once
       9: 
      10: #include <stdint.h>
      11: 
      12: /* Freestanding so the state machine can be stress-tested outside the kernel. */
  ...
      18:     enum : uint32_t {
      19:         kFree = 0,
      20:         kReserved,
      21:         kSubmitted,
      22:         kCompleting,
      23:         kCompleted,
      24:         kDraining
      25:     };
      26: 
      27:     struct Ticket {
      28:         volatile uint32_t state;
      29:         void *packet;
  ...
      63:         return nullptr;
      64:     }
      65: 
      66:     /* Safe for the RTW88 completion callback: bounded atomics only, no
      67:      * allocation, locks, Skywalk calls, or packet release. */
      68:     static bool complete(Ticket *ticket, bool transmitted)
      69:     {
      70:         if (!ticket)
      71:             return false;
      72:         uint32_t expected = kSubmitted;
      73:         if (!__atomic_compare_exchange_n(&ticket->state, &expected,
      74:                                          kCompleting, false,
      75:                                          __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL,
      76:                                          __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE))
      77:             return false;
      78:         ticket->transmitted = transmitted ? 1U : 0U;
      79:         __atomic_store_n(&ticket->state, kCompleted, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
      80:         return true;
      81:     }
      82: 
      83:     /* Single consumer, called from the adapter's deferred/workloop context. */
      84:     Ticket *claimCompleted()
      85:     {
      86:         for (uint32_t i = 0; i < Capacity; ++i) {
      87:             Ticket *ticket = &_slots[i];
      88:             uint32_t expected = kCompleted;
      89:             if (__atomic_compare_exchange_n(&ticket->state, &expected,
      90:                                             kDraining, false,
      91:                                             __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL,
      92:                                             __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE))
      93:                 return ticket;
      94:         }
  ...
     112:     static bool transmitted(const Ticket *ticket)
     113:     {
     114:         return ticket && ticket->transmitted != 0;
     115:     }
     116: 
     117:     bool hasOutstanding() const
     118:     {
     119:         for (uint32_t i = 0; i < Capacity; ++i) {
     120:             if (__atomic_load_n(&_slots[i].state, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE) != kFree)
     121:                 return true;
     122:         }
     123:         return false;
